# Agents · A · part 19

898 entries, AndroidCoroutinesSpecialist to anvil-ip-uspto-drafter. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **AndroidCoroutinesSpecialist**: Specialist for Kotlin Coroutines and Flow scopes in the modern Android multi-module ecosystem. Use when: the scope touches Coroutines, Flow, StateFlow, SharedFlow, suspend functions, channel-based communication, structured concurrency, dispatcher injection, or Turbine-based test assertions. Reduces context of AndroidProgrammer by loading only concurrency-specific skills. Input: Describe the coroutines/Flow change: which StateFlow/SharedFlow, dispatcher concern, or concurrency pattern to implement or fix. ([Andrer757/Kickoff-V2](https://github.com/Andrer757/Kickoff-V2/blob/HEAD/templates/now-in-android/.agents/agents/AndroidCoroutinesSpecialist.md) · ★3)

Reads chunks one-by-one, tracks topic continuity across the group, and emits 0 stamps for continuation chunks. Use when running the anibon-timestamper pipeline — one subagent per group, all groups in parallel. ([ZenitHTH/anibon-stream-synthesis](https://github.com/ZenitHTH/anibon-stream-synthesis/blob/HEAD/agents/anibon-chunk-timestamper.md) · ★1)

Ingests spotter GARBLED_NOTES blocks, coordinates whisper_dispatcher.py to obtain ground-truth audio transcripts via whisper.cpp, applies anti-cascade validation, and syncs confirmed entries into garbled_replacements.json. Use once after all anibon-chunk-timestamper subagents have returned. ([ZenitHTH/anibon-stream-synthesis](https://github.com/ZenitHTH/anibon-stream-synthesis/blob/HEAD/agents/anibon-garbled-notes.md) · ★1)

Has access to all tools. ([ZenitHTH/anibon-stream-synthesis](https://github.com/ZenitHTH/anibon-stream-synthesis/blob/HEAD/agents/anibon-live-service-researcher.md) · ★1)

Use once after all anibon-chunk-timestamper subagents have returned their results. RAG/KG hybrid_query로 근거를 수집하여 Direct Answer(한 문단) + Evidence Chain(3-7개, 각 grounding·confidence·verifiability·verification_sketch) + Open Sub-Questions를 산출한다. ([WhereTheTunnelEnds/quorum](https://github.com/WhereTheTunnelEnds/quorum/blob/HEAD/agents/antigravity-agent.md))

- **antigravity-analyst**: Antigravity analysis lane. Runs Gemini 3.8 Flash (high) via `agy -p` in a read-only git worktree with a JSON verdict schema, on the Google AI pool. Use for system, security-rules, infra, CI, and data-model review, whole-repository reads (switch to Gemini 3.1 Pro only when 1M context is the point), and browser verification through chrome-devtools-mcp. Never writes; never runs against a live tree; reports `unavailable` if agy is missing or the pool is drained. - **anvil-adversarial-reviewer**: Use for independent adversarial review of anvil-serving code, docs, workflow packets, and promotion evidence. ([fakoli/anvil-serving](https://github.com/fakoli/anvil-serving/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/anvil-adversarial-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **anvil-beads**: Evidence-first coding agent built on Beads. Verifies before presenting, attacks its own output with fresh adversarial reviewers, and uses a durable Beads work/evidence graph instead of a SQL verification ledger. ([danieldekay/ai-ops](https://github.com/danieldekay/ai-ops/blob/HEAD/agents/anvil-beads.agent.md) · ★1)
- **anvil-benchmark-runner**: Use for bounded benchmark probes and durable benchmark artifacts after preflight has passed. ([fakoli/anvil-serving](https://github.com/fakoli/anvil-serving/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/anvil-benchmark-runner.md) · ★1)
- **anvil-critic**: Adversarial, read-only critique of an anvil task spec. Verifies every cited file path, walks each acceptance criterion for vagueness, and surfaces undefined behavior, contradictions, deferred decisions, scope creep, and missing context before the implementing agent ever runs. Emits one structured anvil-spec-critique block with BLOCKER/HIGH/MEDIUM/LOW findings. ([tcashel/smithy](https://github.com/tcashel/smithy/blob/HEAD/plugins/anvil/agents/critic.md))
